--- entity_google_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
review_id: 2842
entity_slug: villaggio-grille
google_review_name: places/ChIJbaMnU8MFmogRz0WOaS-DJqQ/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT21STlJrUXRMWEJHZUdWVlgwcFJNbG8wYzFGUGFtYxAB
rating: 3
review_text: I dined alone last night at Villaggio Grill and had a pretty disappointing experience. I started with a blueberry coconut mojito, which was very good.

I ordered the burrata caprese appetizer and then the seared scallop entree, which I was really excited to try. After about 45 minutes, my server came to tell me that the reason my entree was taking so long is because my scallops were “frozen solid”. I appreciate the honest communication, but I would rather not have known my seafood was frozen, though I do understand that is a common occurrence in most restaurants.

At this point, I still had not received my appetizer, so I politely asked for that in the meantime. My entree arrived a few minutes later and I was immediately disappointed with the portion for the price I was paying. The four scallops were rather small, but they were cooked pretty well. The risotto was extremely bland and there was no vegetable side included with the dinner.

After I completed my entree, my appetizer finally arrived and it was the shining star of my entire meal.

As a restaurant manager myself, I would want to know this information, so I did give this feedback to the manager on duty. She was apologetic and offered to remove the appetizer from my meal, which I did not expect but do appreciate. Overall, the experience was disappointing, but I can see how others may enjoy this establishment.

body: We had an absolutely fantastic dining experience at Villagio’s! From start to finish, everything was spot on. The food was incredible—perfectly cooked, beautifully presented, and full of flavor. The fried calamari was crispy and light, the salad was fresh and well-balanced, and the lobster pasta was rich, indulgent, and easily the star of the night. You can really tell care and quality go into every dish.

A huge thank-you to our waiter Luke, who was attentive, friendly, and made sure we had everything we needed without ever feeling rushed. He truly elevated the experience. We also want to give special recognition to Myra, the manager, who checked in on us and made sure everything was perfect—her attentiveness and professionalism did not go unnoticed.

Between the amazing food and the outstanding service, Villagio’s exceeded our expectations. We’ll absolutely be back and will be recommending it to everyone we know!

--- entity_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
id: 56ff8580-e02a-4915-bcfe-ff2456b92674
entity_slug: villaggio-grille
reviewer_name: Shelby Hammer
rating: 3
body: I dined alone last night at Villaggio Grill and had a pretty disappointing experience. I started with a blueberry coconut mojito, which was very good.

I ordered the burrata caprese appetizer and then the seared scallop entree, which I was really excited to try. After about 45 minutes, my server came to tell me that the reason my entree was taking so long is because my scallops were “frozen solid”. I appreciate the honest communication, but I would rather not have known my seafood was frozen, though I do understand that is a common occurrence in most restaurants.

At this point, I still had not received my appetizer, so I politely asked for that in the meantime. My entree arrived a few minutes later and I was immediately disappointed with the portion for the price I was paying. The four scallops were rather small, but they were cooked pretty well. The risotto was extremely bland and there was no vegetable side included with the dinner.

After I completed my entree, my appetizer finally arrived and it was the shining star of my entire meal.

As a restaurant manager myself, I would want to know this information, so I did give this feedback to the manager on duty. She was apologetic and offered to remove the appetizer from my meal, which I did not expect but do appreciate. Overall, the experience was disappointing, but I can see how others may enjoy this establishment.

entity_slug: villaggio-grille
reviewer_name: Britany Purtee
rating: 3
body: This was our first visit to the Gulf Shores area so all of our restaurant picks have been based on reviews. I’m here to offer a review that I don’t see listed. This place is ok. If you’re in the area of the Wharf then sure, it works. But next time I won’t go out of my way to visit this restaurant or the Wharf. None of it seems to be managed well. We sat outside because it was a nice night. Everything seemed dirty and loud. The guys cleaning off tables pushed around a cart with dirty dishes that was exceptionally loud bouncing on the cobblestone ground. And they were cleaning the tables with a rag that was extremely dirty.

The food wasn’t bad. We started with a house salad that was really good and a Caesar salad that was very lacking (it didn’t even have croutons). My husband got the pork chop that was overcooked and dry but the sides were delicious (Mac and cheese and broccolini). My girls had a wood fired pizza that didn’t look like it was baked in a HOT wood fired oven. My oysters were very flavorful but they were tiny! All in all it didn’t have the ambiance or quality that we were expecting.
